% gen_roulette.py network 1000
% Roulette network.rkt: message 1111 through diamonds of a noisy node (flip 0.01: one bit flipped,
% chosen uniformly) and a forwarding one (flip 0.99: passed, else lost), split by flip 0.5.
% The message as its four bits b(I,B) and l(I) for lost; t(I,B) flips bit B in diamond I.

t(1,3) :- sp(1), nz(1), bit(1,3).
l(1) :- \+sp(1), \+fw(1).
t(2,0) :- sp(2), nz(2), bit(2,0).
t(2,1) :- sp(2), nz(2), bit(2,1).
t(2,2) :- sp(2), nz(2), bit(2,2).
t(2,3) :- sp(2), nz(2), bit(2,3).
b(2,0) :- \+t(1,0), \+t(2,0).
b(2,0) :- t(1,0), t(2,0).
b(2,1) :- \+t(1,1), \+t(2,1).
b(2,1) :- t(1,1), t(2,1).
b(2,2) :- \+t(1,2), \+t(2,2).
